│                                                                              │
│ stan_code = r""" functions { matrix cov_ard_sqexp(matrix X, vector rho, real │
│ alpha, real jitter) { int N = rows(X); int D = cols(X); matrix[N, N] K;      │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  for (i in 1:N) {                                                            │
│    K[i, i] = square(alpha) + jitter;                                         │
│    for (j in (i + 1):N) {                                                    │
│      real s = 0;                                                             │
│      for (d in 1:D) {                                                        │
│        real t = (X[i, d] - X[j, d]) / rho[d];                                │
│        s += t * t;                                                           │
│      }                                                                       │
│      K[i, j] = square(alpha) * exp(-0.5 * s);                                │
│      K[j, i] = K[i, j];                                                      │
│    }                                                                         │
│  }                                                                           │
│  return K;                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ matrix cross_cov_ard(matrix X, matrix X_new, vector rho, real alpha) { int N │
│ = rows(X); int M = rows(X_new); int D = cols(X); // == cols(X_new) matrix[N, │
│ M] Kx; for (i in 1:N) { for (j in 1:M) { real s = 0; for (d in 1:D) { real t │
│ = (X[i, d] - X_new[j, d]) / rho[d]; s += t * t; } Kx[i, j] = square(alpha) * │
│ exp(-0.5 * s); } } return Kx; } }                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ data { int<lower=1> N; int<lower=1> D; matrix[N, D] X; int<lower=1> P;       │
│ matrix[N, P] H; vector[N] y; real<lower=0> jitter;                           │
│                                                                              │
│ int<lower=0> N_new; matrix[N_new, D] X_new; matrix[N_new, P] H_new; }        │
│                                                                              │
│ parameters { vector[P] beta; real<lower=0> alpha; vector<lower=0>[D] rho;    │
│ real<lower=0> sigma; }                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│ transformed parameters { vector[N] mu = H * beta; matrix[N, N] K =           │
│ cov_ard_sqexp(X, rho, alpha, jitter) + diag_matrix(rep_vector(square(sigma), │
│ N)); matrix[N, N] L_K = cholesky_decompose(K); }                             │
│                                                                              │
│ model { beta  ~ normal(0, 1); alpha ~ normal(0, 1.5); rho   ~ lognormal(0,   │
│ 1); sigma ~ normal(0, 0.5); y ~ multi_normal_cholesky(mu, L_K); }            │
│                                                                              │
│ generated quantities { vector[N_new] y_new; if (N_new > 0) { matrix[N,       │
│ N_new] K_x_xnew = cross_cov_ard(X, X_new, rho, alpha); matrix[N_new, N_new]  │
│ K_new = cov_ard_sqexp(X_new, rho, alpha, jitter); vector[N] resid = y - (H * │
│ beta);                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  vector[N] alpha_vec = mdivide_left_tri_low(L_K, resid);                     │
│  alpha_vec = mdivide_left_tri_low(L_K&#x27;, alpha_vec);                     │
│                                                                              │
│  vector[N_new] f_mean = (K_x_xnew&#x27; * alpha_vec);                        │
│  matrix[N, N_new] v = mdivide_left_tri_low(L_K, K_x_xnew);                   │
│  vector[N_new] f_var = to_vector(diagonal(K_new)) -                          │
│  to_vector(columns_dot_self(v));                                             │
│  for (m in 1:N_new) {                                                        │
│    real m_mean = (H_new[m] * beta) + f_mean[m];                              │
│    real m_sd   = sqrt(f_var[m] + square(sigma));                             │
│    y_new[m] = normal_rng(m_mean, m_sd);                                      │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ } } """                                                                      │

│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │

│                                                                              │
│ meta = json.load(open('/app/meta_public.json','r')) train_X =                │

│                                                                              │
│ P = int(meta['P']) jitter = float(meta['jitter'])                            │

│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                  H uses intercept and first two columns                  ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ H = np.column_stack((np.ones(N), train_X[:, 0], train_X[:, 1])) if N_new >   │
│ 0: H_new = np.column_stack((np.ones(N_new), test_X[:, 0], test_X[:, 1]))     │

│ data = { 'N': N, 'D': D, 'X': train_X, 'P': P, 'H': H, 'y': train_y,         │
│ 'jitter': jitter, 'N_new': N_new, 'X_new': test_X if N_new > 0 else          │
│ np.zeros((0, D)), 'H_new': H_new if N_new > 0 else np.zeros((0, P)) }        │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │

│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃            Prepare initial values approximating init_r = 0.1             ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ rng = np.random.RandomState(1) num_chains = 4 inits = [] for c in            │
│ range(num_chains): init = {} init['beta'] = rng.normal(scale=0.1,            │
│ size=P).tolist() init['alpha'] = float(abs(rng.normal(scale=0.1))) + 1e-6    │
│ init['rho'] = (abs(rng.normal(scale=0.1, size=D)) + 1e-6).tolist()           │
│ init['sigma'] = float(abs(rng.normal(scale=0.1))) + 1e-6 inits.append(init)  │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃ Sampling hyperparameters to mirror R's sampling (iter=2000, warmup=1000, ┃ │
│ ┃                        thin=2 -> num_samples=500)                        ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ print('Starting sampling...') fit = posterior.sample( num_chains=num_chains, │

│ ┃             rho columns are like 'rho[1]','rho[2]','rho[3]'              ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │

│ <output> import json import numpy as np import pandas as pd import stan      │
│                                                                              │
│ stan_code = r""" functions { matrix cov_ard_sqexp(matrix X, vector rho, real │
│ alpha, real jitter) { int N = rows(X); int D = cols(X); matrix[N, N] K;      │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  for (i in 1:N) {                                                            │
│    K[i, i] = square(alpha) + jitter;                                         │
│    for (j in (i + 1):N) {                                                    │
│      real s = 0;                                                             │
│      for (d in 1:D) {                                                        │
│        real t = (X[i, d] - X[j, d]) / rho[d];                                │
│        s += t * t;                                                           │
│      }                                                                       │
│      K[i, j] = square(alpha) * exp(-0.5 * s);                                │
│      K[j, i] = K[i, j];                                                      │
│    }                                                                         │
│  }                                                                           │
│  return K;                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ matrix cross_cov_ard(matrix X, matrix X_new, vector rho, real alpha) { int N │
│ = rows(X); int M = rows(X_new); int D = cols(X); // == cols(X_new) matrix[N, │
│ M] Kx; for (i in 1:N) { for (j in 1:M) { real s = 0; for (d in 1:D) { real t │
│ = (X[i, d] - X_new[j, d]) / rho[d]; s += t * t; } Kx[i, j] = square(alpha) * │
│ exp(-0.5 * s); } } return Kx; } }                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ data { int<lower=1> N; int<lower=1> D; matrix[N, D] X; int<lower=1> P;       │
│ matrix[N, P] H; vector[N] y; real<lower=0> jitter;                           │
│                                                                              │
│ int<lower=0> N_new; matrix[N_new, D] X_new; matrix[N_new, P] H_new; }        │
│                                                                              │
│ parameters { vector[P] beta; real<lower=0> alpha; vector<lower=0>[D] rho;    │
│ real<lower=0> sigma; }                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│ transformed parameters { vector[N] mu = H * beta; matrix[N, N] K =           │
│ cov_ard_sqexp(X, rho, alpha, jitter) + diag_matrix(rep_vector(square(sigma), │
│ N)); matrix[N, N] L_K = cholesky_decompose(K); }                             │
│                                                                              │
│ model { beta  ~ normal(0, 1); alpha ~ normal(0, 1.5); rho   ~ lognormal(0,   │
│ 1); sigma ~ normal(0, 0.5); y ~ multi_normal_cholesky(mu, L_K); }            │
│                                                                              │
│ generated quantities { vector[N_new] y_new; if (N_new > 0) { matrix[N,       │
│ N_new] K_x_xnew = cross_cov_ard(X, X_new, rho, alpha); matrix[N_new, N_new]  │
│ K_new = cov_ard_sqexp(X_new, rho, alpha, jitter); vector[N] resid = y - (H * │
│ beta);                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  vector[N] alpha_vec = mdivide_left_tri_low(L_K, resid);                     │
│  alpha_vec = mdivide_left_tri_low(L_K&#x27;, alpha_vec);                     │
│                                                                              │
│  vector[N_new] f_mean = (K_x_xnew&#x27; * alpha_vec);                        │
│  matrix[N, N_new] v = mdivide_left_tri_low(L_K, K_x_xnew);                   │
│  vector[N_new] f_var = to_vector(diagonal(K_new)) -                          │
│  to_vector(columns_dot_self(v));                                             │
│  for (m in 1:N_new) {                                                        │
│    real m_mean = (H_new[m] * beta) + f_mean[m];                              │
│    real m_sd   = sqrt(f_var[m] + square(sigma));                             │
│    y_new[m] = normal_rng(m_mean, m_sd);                                      │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ } } """                                                                      │

│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │

│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │

│ vector[N_new] y_new;\n  if (N_new > 0) {\n    matrix[N, N_new] K_x_xnew =    │
│ cross_cov_ard(X, X_new, rho, alpha);\n    matrix[N_new, N_new] K_new =       │
│ cov_ard_sqexp(X_new, rho, alpha, jitter);\n    vector[N] resid = y - (H *    │
│ beta);\n\n    vector[N] alpha_vec = mdivide_left_tri_low(L_K, resid);\n      │
│ alpha_vec = mdivide_left_tri_low(L_K&#x27;, alpha_vec);\n\n    vector[N_new] │
│ f_mean = (K_x_xnew&#x27; * alpha_vec);\n    matrix[N, N_new] v =             │
│ mdivide_left_tri_low(L_K, K_x_xnew);\n    vector[N_new] f_var =              │
│ to_vector(diagonal(K_new)) - to_vector(columns_dot_self(v));\n    for (m in  │
│ 1:N_new) {\n      real m_mean = (H_new[m] * beta) + f_mean[m];\n      real   │
│ m_sd   = sqrt(f_var[m] + square(sigma));\n      y_new[m] =                   │
│ normal_rng(m_mean, m_sd);\n    }\n  }\n}\n"""\n\n# Load data\nmeta =         │
│ json.load(open(&#x27;/app/meta_public.json&#x27;,&#x27;r&#x27;))\ntrain_X =  │
│ pd.read_csv(&#x27;/app/train_X.csv&#x27;).values\ntest_X =                   │
│ pd.read_csv(&#x27;/app/test_X.csv&#x27;).values\ntrain_y =                   │
│ pd.read_csv(&#x27;/app/train_y.csv&#x27;)[&#x27;y&#x27;].values\n\nP =       │
│ int(meta[&#x27;P&#x27;])\njitter = float(meta[&#x27;jitter&#x27;])\n\nN, D = │
│ train_X.shape\nN_new = test_X.shape[0]\n\n# H uses intercept and first two   │
│ columns\nH = np.column_stack((np.ones(N), train_X[:, 0], train_X[:, 1]))\nif │
│ N_new > 0:\n    H_new = np.column_stack((np.ones(N_new), test_X[:, 0],       │
│ test_X[:, 1]))\nelse:\n    H_new = np.zeros((0, P))\n\ndata = {\n            │
│ &#x27;N&#x27;: N,\n    &#x27;D&#x27;: D,\n    &#x27;X&#x27;: train_X,\n      │
│ &#x27;P&#x27;: P,\n    &#x27;H&#x27;: H,\n    &#x27;y&#x27;: train_y,\n      │
│ &#x27;jitter&#x27;: jitter,\n    &#x27;N_new&#x27;: N_new,\n                 │
│ &#x27;X_new&#x27;: test_X if N_new > 0 else np.zeros((0, D)),\n              │
│ &#x27;H_new&#x27;: H_new if N_new > 0 else np.zeros((0, P))\n}\n\n# Build    │
│ model with specified random seed\nprint(&#x27;Building Stan                  │
│ model...&#x27;)\nposterior = stan.build(stan_code, data=data,                │
│ random_seed=1)\nprint(&#x27;Model built.&#x27;)\n\n# Prepare initial values  │
│ approximating init_r = 0.1\nrng = np.random.RandomState(1)\nnum_chains =     │
│ 4\ninits = []\nfor c in range(num_chains):\n    init = {}\n                  │
│ init[&#x27;beta&#x27;] = rng.normal(scale=0.1, size=P).tolist()\n            │
│ init[&#x27;alpha&#x27;] = float(abs(rng.normal(scale=0.1))) + 1e-6\n         │
│ init[&#x27;rho&#x27;] = (abs(rng.normal(scale=0.1, size=D)) +                │
│ 1e-6).tolist()\n    init[&#x27;sigma&#x27;] =                                │
│ float(abs(rng.normal(scale=0.1))) + 1e-6\n    inits.append(init)\n\n#        │
│ Sampling hyperparameters to mirror R&#x27;s sampling (iter=2000,             │
│ warmup=1000, thin=2 -> num_samples=500)\nprint(&#x27;Starting                │
│ sampling...&#x27;)\n# Try to include adapt_delta and max_treedepth; fall     │
│ back if backend rejects them\ntry:\n    fit = posterior.sample(\n            │
│ num_chains=num_chains,\n                           num_warmup=1000,\n        │
│ num_samples=500,\n                           seed=1,\n                       │
│ adapt_delta=0.93,\n                           max_treedepth=14,\n            │
│ init=inits)\nexcept TypeError:\n    # some backends might not accept         │
│ adapt_delta or max_treedepth as kwargs\n    try:\n        fit =              │
│ posterior.sample(\n                               num_chains=num_chains,\n   │
│ num_warmup=1000,\n                               num_samples=500,\n          │
│ seed=1,\n                               init=inits)\n    except Exception as │
│ e:\n        print(&#x27;Sampling failed:&#x27;, e)\n                         │
│ raise\nprint(&#x27;Sampling completed.&#x27;)\n\n# Convert to                │
│ DataFrame\ntry:\n    df = fit.to_frame()\nexcept Exception:\n    df =        │
│ fit.to_dataframe()\n\n# Extract posterior means\nalpha_mean =                │
│ df[&#x27;alpha&#x27;].mean()\nsigma_mean = df[&#x27;sigma&#x27;].mean()\n#   │
│ rho columns are like                                                         │
│ &#x27;rho[1]&#x27;,&#x27;rho[2]&#x27;,&#x27;rho[3]&#x27;\nrho_cols = [col    │
│ for col in df.columns if col.startswith(&#x27;rho&#x27;)]\n# Sort rho        │
│ columns by index\nrho_cols = sorted(rho_cols, key=lambda s:                  │
│ int(s.split(&#x27;[&#x27;)[1].split(&#x27;]&#x27;)[0]))\nrho_means =         │
│ df[rho_cols].mean().values\nbeta_cols = [col for col in df.columns if        │
│ col.startswith(&#x27;beta&#x27;)]\nbeta_cols = sorted(beta_cols, key=lambda  │
│ s: int(s.split(&#x27;[&#x27;)[1].split(&#x27;]&#x27;)[0]))\nbeta_means =     │
│ df[beta_cols].mean().values\n\n# Save to CSVs (numeric values                │
│ only)\nnp.savetxt(&#x27;/app/alpha_est.csv&#x27;, np.array([alpha_mean]),    │
│ fmt=&#x27;%.18e&#x27;)\nnp.savetxt(&#x27;/app/sigma_est.csv&#x27;,           │
│ np.array([sigma_mean]),                                                      │
│ fmt=&#x27;%.18e&#x27;)\nnp.savetxt(&#x27;/app/rho_est.csv&#x27;,             │
│ rho_means.reshape(-1,1),                                                     │
│ fmt=&#x27;%.18e&#x27;)\nnp.savetxt(&#x27;/app/beta_est.csv&#x27;,            │
│ beta_means.reshape(-1,1), fmt=&#x27;%.18e&#x27;)\n\nprint(&#x27;Saved        │
│ posterior means to CSV files.&#x27;)\nPY'}                                   │
